+"""FROZEN REFERENCE: harness/detect.py as of f756095, before the Apple Silicon optimisation (8 Nov 2026). Do not edit.
+tests/test_guard_equivalence.py and tools/mac_bench.py (guard_ref) compare the live detector against it.
+
+Local colour-blob detector for the relay table: golf balls, the black 8-balls, blocks and cylinders.
+
+It runs on the laptop in a few milliseconds per frame, with no API call, so it serves two jobs the Claude monitor
+is too slow or too fragile for:
+
+1. A fast black-ball guard. Every harness poll on a guarded stage looks at the wrist cameras; a black ball between
+   the fingers counts as a guard "yes" at once. This also keeps the guard answering when the Claude API is down.
+2. Boxes on the frames the monitor sees, labelled with class, colour and (for cylinders) upright/lying, so the
+   monitor counts balls in the bin, black balls and tower blocks from marked objects instead of guessing.
+
+It does NOT change what Dream-1 sees or is told: the station builds the policy's camera input itself, the fine-tune
+was trained on plain frames, and prompts must stay the recorded task texts word for word.
+
+Colours live in a YAML file (plans/table_colors.yaml). Tune them on a real frame of the table:
+    python -m harness.detect plans/table_colors.yaml frame.jpg --out boxed.jpg
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import io
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import numpy as np
+import yaml
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+try:
+    from scipy import ndimage as _ndi
+except ImportError:   # pragma: no cover
+    _ndi = None
+
+SMALL_BALL_FRAC = 0.004  # a golf ball in the overhead view is ~0.05-0.1% of the image
+WORK_W = 640           # frames are shrunk to this width before detection
+COLORS = {"avoid": (230, 20, 20), "target": (20, 200, 60), "info": (30, 120, 230)}
+
+
+@dataclass
+class ClassSpec:
+    name: str
+    hsv_lo: tuple[int, int, int]
+    hsv_hi: tuple[int, int, int]
+    min_frac: float = 0.0005       # blob area as a fraction of the image
+    max_frac: float = 0.15
+    label: str = ""                # colour name, e.g. "blue"
+    # which shapes of this colour to report, and how: kind (ball | cube | stick) -> role (avoid | target | info).
+    # Empty = every kind, role info. Black lists only ball, so the black gripper fingers are never reported.
+    kinds: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
+    small_ball: bool = False       # this colour only comes as balls: read a small compact blob as a ball
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Box:
+    cls: str
+    x0: float
+    y0: float
+    x1: float
+    y1: float                      # fractions of the image, 0-1
+    frac: float
+    orientation: str | None = None
+    role: str = "info"
+
+    @property
+    def cx(self) -> float:
+        return (self.x0 + self.x1) / 2
+
+    @property
+    def cy(self) -> float:
+        return (self.y0 + self.y1) / 2
+
+    def text(self) -> str:
+        return self.cls + (f" {self.orientation}" if self.orientation else "")
+
+
+@dataclass
+class DetectConfig:
+    classes: list[ClassSpec]
+    guard_class: str = "black_ball"
+    wrist_roles: list[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["wrist"])
+    grip_roi: tuple[float, float, float, float] = (0.25, 0.35, 0.75, 1.0)   # x0 y0 x1 y1 between the fingers
+    grip_min_frac: float = 0.01   # a ball this big in the wrist view is in or at the fingers
+    table_roi: tuple[float, float, float, float] | None = None   # overhead view: ignore blobs outside (people, chairs)
+    annotate: bool = True
+
+
+def load_config(path: str | Path) -> DetectConfig:
+    raw = yaml.safe_load(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
+    classes = []
+    for name, c in (raw.get("classes") or {}).items():
+        lo, hi = c["hsv"]
+        classes.append(ClassSpec(name=name, hsv_lo=tuple(lo), hsv_hi=tuple(hi),
+                                 min_frac=float(c.get("min_frac", 0.0005)), max_frac=float(c.get("max_frac", 0.15)),
+                                 label=c.get("label", name), kinds=dict(c.get("kinds") or {}),
+                                 small_ball=bool(c.get("small_ball", False))))
+    g = raw.get("guard") or {}
+    return DetectConfig(classes=classes, guard_class=g.get("class", "black_ball"),
+                        wrist_roles=list(g.get("wrist_roles", ["wrist"])),
+                        grip_roi=tuple(g.get("grip_roi", (0.25, 0.35, 0.75, 1.0))),
+                        grip_min_frac=float(g.get("grip_min_frac", 0.01)), annotate=bool(raw.get("annotate", True)),
+                        table_roi=tuple(raw["table_roi"]) if raw.get("table_roi") else None)
+
+
+def _load(jpeg: bytes, min_w: int | None = None) -> Image.Image | None:
+    """Decoded RGB image, or None for a bad frame. With min_w, a JPEG is decoded at the smallest 1/2, 1/4 or 1/8
+    scale that is still at least min_w wide (libjpeg's draft mode): a 1280 px station frame decodes at 640."""
+    try:
+        im = Image.open(io.BytesIO(jpeg))
+        if min_w and im.format == "JPEG" and im.width > min_w:
+            im.draft("RGB", (min_w, max(1, im.height * min_w // im.width)))
+        im.load()
+        return im.convert("RGB")
+    except Exception:   # noqa: BLE001 - a bad frame is simply not looked at
+        return None
+
+
+def _hsv(im: Image.Image) -> np.ndarray:
+    w, h = im.size
+    small = im.resize((WORK_W, max(1, round(h * WORK_W / w))))
+    return np.asarray(small.convert("HSV"), dtype=np.int16)
+
+
+def _label(mask: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
+    if _ndi is not None:
+        return _ndi.label(mask)
+    lab = np.zeros(mask.shape, dtype=np.int32)   # small 4-connected flood fill for machines without scipy
+    n = 0
+    for y, x in zip(*np.nonzero(mask)):
+        if lab[y, x]:
+            continue
+        n += 1
+        stack = [(y, x)]
+        lab[y, x] = n
+        while stack:
+            cy, cx = stack.pop()
+            for ny, nx in ((cy + 1, cx), (cy - 1, cx), (cy, cx + 1), (cy, cx - 1)):
+                if 0 <= ny < mask.shape[0] and 0 <= nx < mask.shape[1] and mask[ny, nx] and not lab[ny, nx]:
+                    lab[ny, nx] = n
+                    stack.append((ny, nx))
+    return lab, n
+
+
+def _in_range(hsv: np.ndarray, lo: tuple[int, int, int], hi: tuple[int, int, int]) -> np.ndarray:
+    h, s, v = hsv[..., 0], hsv[..., 1], hsv[..., 2]
+    hue = (h >= lo[0]) & (h <= hi[0]) if lo[0] <= hi[0] else (h >= lo[0]) | (h <= hi[0])   # red wraps around
+    return hue & (s >= lo[1]) & (s <= hi[1]) & (v >= lo[2]) & (v <= hi[2])
+
+
+def _kind(ys: np.ndarray, xs: np.ndarray) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
+    """(kind, orientation) of one blob from its pixels, independent of rotation.
+    ball: compact and fills its circumscribed circle; cube: compact but square (or an upright stick seen from above);
+    stick: long and thin, i.e. lying on its side."""
+    n = len(xs)
+    if n < 6:
+        return None, None
+    pts = np.stack([xs, ys], axis=1).astype(float)
+    c = pts.mean(axis=0)
+    ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(np.cov((pts - c).T)) if n > 2 else np.array([1.0, 1.0])
+    elong = float(np.sqrt(max(ev[1], 1e-9) / max(ev[0], 1e-9)))
+    rmax = float(np.sqrt(((pts - c) ** 2).sum(axis=1).max())) + 0.5
+    roundness = n / (np.pi * rmax * rmax)          # disc ~1, square ~0.64, thin bar small
+    if elong >= 2.2:
+        return "stick", "lying"
+    if elong <= 1.5 and roundness >= 0.72:
+        return "ball", None
+    if elong <= 1.6:
+        return "cube", None
+    return None, None
+
+
+def detect(jpeg: bytes, cfg: DetectConfig, *, overhead: bool = False) -> list[Box]:
+    im = _load(jpeg, WORK_W)
+    return [] if im is None else detect_image(im, cfg, overhead=overhead)
+
+
+def detect_image(im: Image.Image, cfg: DetectConfig, *, overhead: bool = False,
+                 classes: list[ClassSpec] | None = None) -> list[Box]:
+    """detect() on a decoded image; `classes` limits the colours looked for (default: all of cfg's)."""
+    hsv = _hsv(im)
+    H, W = hsv.shape[:2]
+    total = float(H * W)
+    out: list[Box] = []
+    for spec in cfg.classes if classes is None else classes:
+        mask = _in_range(hsv, spec.hsv_lo, spec.hsv_hi)
+        if _ndi is not None:   # close specular highlights and the cubes' holes, so shapes read whole
+            mask = _ndi.binary_fill_holes(_ndi.binary_closing(mask, iterations=2))
+        lab, n = _label(mask)
+        if n == 0:
+            continue
+        sizes = np.bincount(lab.ravel())
+        for i in range(1, n + 1):
+            area = sizes[i]
+            frac = area / total
+            if not spec.min_frac <= frac <= spec.max_frac:
+                continue
+            ys, xs = np.nonzero(lab == i)
+            kind, orient = _kind(ys, xs)
+            if kind == "cube" and spec.small_ball and frac <= SMALL_BALL_FRAC:
+                kind = "ball"   # a colour that only comes as balls: a small, half-lit ball reads square-ish
+                                # (the size cap keeps the white bin out; a held ball fills the wrist view and is round)
+            if kind is None or (spec.kinds and kind not in spec.kinds):
+                continue
+            role = spec.kinds.get(kind, "info")
+            label = f"{spec.label or spec.name} {kind}"
+            out.append(Box(label, xs.min() / W, ys.min() / H, (xs.max() + 1) / W, (ys.max() + 1) / H, frac,
+                           orient, role))
+    if overhead and cfg.table_roi:
+        x0, y0, x1, y1 = cfg.table_roi
+        out = [b for b in out if x0 <= b.cx <= x1 and y0 <= b.cy <= y1]
+    return out
+
+
+def held(boxes: list[Box], cfg: DetectConfig) -> Box | None:
+    """A ball the plan must never bin (role avoid) between the fingers of a wrist camera, big enough to be held."""
+    rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1 = cfg.grip_roi
+    for b in boxes:
+        if b.role == "avoid" and b.frac >= cfg.grip_min_frac and rx0 <= b.cx <= rx1 and ry0 <= b.cy <= ry1:
+            return b
+    return None
+
+
+def annotate(jpeg: bytes, boxes: list[Box]) -> bytes:
+    im = _load(jpeg)
+    if im is None or not boxes:
+        return jpeg
+    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
+    W, H = im.size
+    width = max(2, W // 200)
+    for b in boxes:
+        col = COLORS.get(b.role, COLORS["info"])
+        xy = (b.x0 * W, b.y0 * H, b.x1 * W, b.y1 * H)
+        d.rectangle(xy, outline=col, width=width)
+        d.text((xy[0] + 2, max(0, xy[1] - 12)), b.text().upper() if b.role == "avoid" else b.text(), fill=col)
+    buf = io.BytesIO()
+    im.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=88)
+    return buf.getvalue()
+
+
+class TableDetector:
+    """What the runner and the monitor use."""
+
+    def __init__(self, cfg: DetectConfig) -> None:
+        self.cfg = cfg
+        self.cable_ends = None   # harness.cable_end.CableEndClassifier: USB-C / USB-A end boxes (--cable-ends)
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_file(cls, path: str | Path) -> TableDetector:
+        return cls(load_config(path))
+
+    def _overhead(self, role: str) -> bool:
+        return not any(w in role for w in self.cfg.wrist_roles)
+
+    def boxes(self, frames: dict[str, bytes]) -> dict[str, list[Box]]:
+        return {role: detect(j, self.cfg, overhead=self._overhead(role)) for role, j in frames.items()}
+
+    def guard(self, frames: dict[str, bytes]) -> tuple[bool | None, str]:
+        """(True, why) if a wrist camera shows the guard class between the fingers; (False, ...) if the wrist views
+        were readable and showed none; (None, ...) if no wrist view could be read."""
+        looked = False
+        avoid = [c for c in self.cfg.classes if "avoid" in c.kinds.values()]   # only the colours that can trip it
+        for role, jpeg in frames.items():
+            if not any(w in role for w in self.cfg.wrist_roles):
+                continue
+            im = _load(jpeg, WORK_W)
+            if im is None:
+                continue
+            looked = True
+            b = held(detect_image(im, self.cfg, classes=avoid), self.cfg)
+            if b is not None:
+                return True, f"local detector: {b.cls} between the fingers in {role} ({b.frac:.1%} of the view)"
+        return (False, "local detector: none held") if looked else (None, "local detector: no wrist view")
+
+    def annotate_frames(self, frames: dict[str, bytes]) -> dict[str, bytes]:
+        if not self.cfg.annotate:
+            return frames
+        out = {role: annotate(j, detect(j, self.cfg, overhead=self._overhead(role))) for role, j in frames.items()}
+        if self.cable_ends is not None:
+            out = {role: self.cable_ends.annotate(j) for role, j in out.items()}
+        return out
+
+    def legend(self) -> str:
+        avoid = [f"{c.label or c.name} {k}" for c in self.cfg.classes for k, r in c.kinds.items() if r == "avoid"]
+        return ("Boxes on the images come from a local colour detector and can miss or mislabel objects: "
+                f"RED boxes = {', '.join(avoid) or 'objects to avoid'} (never pick these); green = targets; "
+                "blue = other objects (blocks: colour, cylinders: upright or lying)."
+                + (" " + self.cable_ends.legend() if self.cable_ends is not None else ""))
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run the table detector on one image and draw the boxes.")
+    ap.add_argument("config")
+    ap.add_argument("image")
+    ap.add_argument("--out", default="boxed.jpg")
+    ap.add_argument("--wrist", action="store_true", help="the image is a wrist view (no table_roi)")
+    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
+    det = TableDetector.from_file(args.config)
+    jpeg = Path(args.image).read_bytes()
+    boxes = detect(jpeg, det.cfg, overhead=not args.wrist)
+    for b in boxes:
+        print(f"{b.text():24s} x {b.x0:.2f}-{b.x1:.2f} y {b.y0:.2f}-{b.y1:.2f} area {b.frac:.2%}")
+    Path(args.out).write_bytes(annotate(jpeg, boxes))
+    print(f"{len(boxes)} boxes -> {args.out}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def no_network(monkeypatch):
+    def blocked(*args, **kwargs):
+        raise AssertionError("network is forbidden in these tests")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", blocked)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", KEY)

+@pytest.fixture
+def plan():

+def response(done=False, guard=False, cost=.01, text=None):
+    answer = text if text is not None else '<json>' + json.dumps({
+        "stage_done": done, "guard": guard, "confidence": .95, "reason": "offline"}) + '</json>'
+    return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": answer}}],
+            "usage": {"cost": cost, "prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 20}}
+
+
+def fake_http(monkeypatch, fn):
+    def send(request, timeout):
+        assert request.full_url == "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions"
+        assert request.get_header("Authorization") == f"Bearer {KEY}"
+        assert KEY not in request.data.decode()
+        return io.BytesIO(json.dumps(fn(json.loads(request.data))).encode())
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", send)
+

+def test_translation_preserves_system_text_images_and_wire_fields():
+    image = {"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": "image/jpeg",
+                                         "data": base64.b64encode(JPEG).decode()}}
+    payload = {"system": [{"type": "text", "text": "station rules"}], "max_tokens": 4000,
+               "output_config": {"effort": "low"}, "_frames_sig": "private bookkeeping",
+               "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "overhead"}, image,
+                                                           {"type": "text", "text": "question"}]},
+                            {"role": "assistant", "content": "prior text"}]}
+    body = openrouter_request_body("vision/model", payload)
+    assert bench.validate_translation(payload, body) == 1
+    assert body["messages"][0] == {"role": "system", "content": "station rules"}
+    broken = copy.deepcopy(body)
+    broken["messages"][1]["content"].reverse()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="translation mismatch"):
+        bench.validate_translation(payload, broken)

+def test_raw_stage_answer_survives_monitor_confidence_gate():
+    verdict = parse_monitor_reply('<json>{"stage_done": true, "confidence": 0.2}</json>', 0)
+    assert verdict.stage_done is True and verdict.done is None
+    negative = parse_monitor_reply('<json>{"stage_done": false, "confidence": 0.9}</json>', 0)
+    assert negative.stage_done is False and negative.done is None
+    assert parse_monitor_reply("broken", 0).stage_done is None

+def test_race_collects_loser_cost_after_winning_latency(plan, monkeypatch):
+    release_loser = threading.Event()
+    slow_finished = threading.Event()
+    def respond(body):
+        if body["model"] == "slow/model":
+            assert release_loser.wait(5)
+            slow_finished.set()
+            return response(cost=.07)
+        return response(cost=.01)
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, respond)
+    original = bench.Accounting.drain
+    def drain(self, expected, timeout_s):
+        assert not slow_finished.is_set()  # check_now returned while the loser was still waiting
+        release_loser.set()
+        return original(self, expected, timeout_s)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bench.Accounting, "drain", drain)
+    row = bench.run_trial(plan, bench.Judge("openrouter", ("fast/model", "slow/model")),
+                          ("photo", "ball_1", False, False), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,
+                          dry_run=False, timeout_s=5)
+    assert slow_finished.is_set() and row["drained"]
+    assert row["stage_correct"] and row["guard_correct"]
+    assert row["cost_usd"] == pytest.approx(.08)
+    assert row["attempts_finished"] == 2
+    summary = bench.summaries([row])[0]
+    assert summary["cost_per_call_usd"] == pytest.approx(.04)
+    assert summary["cost_per_trial_usd"] == pytest.approx(.08)
+

+def test_real_monitor_hedge_recovers_from_bad_json_and_counts_every_copy(plan, monkeypatch):
+    lock = threading.Lock()
+    counter = 0
+    def respond(body):
+        nonlocal counter
+        with lock:
+            counter += 1
+            n = counter
+        return response(cost=.02, text="bad JSON" if n == 1 else None)
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, respond)
+    row = bench.run_trial(plan, bench.Judge("openrouter", ("vision/model",), 2),
+                          ("photo", "ball_1", False, False), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,
+                          dry_run=False, timeout_s=5)
+    assert row["stage_correct"] and not row["parse_failure"]
+    assert counter == row["attempts_finished"] == 2
+    assert row["cost_usd"] == pytest.approx(.04)
+    assert plan.claude_user.get("hedge", 1) == 1

+def test_race_inside_hedge_counts_all_four_attempts_and_positive_guard(plan, monkeypatch):
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, lambda body: response(guard=True, cost=.02))
+    row = bench.run_trial(plan, bench.Judge("openrouter", ("first/model", "second/model"), 2),
+                          ("sim_eight_held", "ball_1", False, True), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,

+    assert row["attempts_expected"] == row["attempts_finished"] == 4
+    assert row["cost_usd"] == pytest.approx(.08)
+
+

+        send({"messages": [], "max_tokens": 100})
+    assert len(events) == 1 and events[0]["error_type"] == "ValueError"
+
+
+def test_malformed_usage_stops_without_callback_failure(plan, monkeypatch):
+    def respond(body):
+        reply = response()

+        return reply
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, respond)

+                          ("photo", "ball_1", False, False), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,
+                          dry_run=False, timeout_s=5)
+    assert row["stage_correct"] and row["drained"]
+    assert not row["cost_complete"] and row["cost_usd"] is None
+

+    monkeypatch.setattr(bench, "anthropic_transport", make)

+    assert not row["cost_complete"] and row["cost_usd"] is None

+"""harness.cable_end: ferrule candidates and the USB-C / USB-A classifier."""
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import cv2
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from harness.cable_end import CableEndClassifier, crop, ferrules
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+MODEL = ROOT / "models" / "cable_end.onnx"
+DATA = Path(__file__).parent / "data"
+ORANGE = (0, 70, 235)   # BGR, the anodised ferrule
+
+
+def _table():
+    return np.full((360, 640, 3), (200, 205, 208), np.uint8)
+
+
+def test_a_ferrule_is_a_candidate_and_a_cable_run_is_not():
+    im = _table()
+    cv2.rectangle(im, (300, 160), (340, 182), ORANGE, -1)        # ferrule: compact, filled
+    cv2.line(im, (20, 300), (600, 330), ORANGE, 4)               # cable: long and thin
+    c = ferrules(im)
+    assert len(c) == 1 and abs(c[0]["cx"] - 320) < 3 and abs(c[0]["cy"] - 171) < 3
+    assert c[0]["side"] > 40 * 2                                  # the crop keeps the tip and the relief in view
+    assert crop(im, c[0]).shape == (96, 96, 3)
+
+
+def test_no_orange_no_candidates():
+    assert ferrules(_table()) == []
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_photo_ends_are_labelled():
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL, min_prob=0.5)
+    for name, want in (("cable_usbc_end.jpg", "usb_c"), ("cable_usba_end.jpg", "usb_a")):
+        ends = clf.ends_jpeg((DATA / name).read_bytes())
+        assert ends, name
+        best = max(ends, key=lambda e: e.prob)
+        assert best.label == want, (name, [(e.label, round(e.prob, 2)) for e in ends])
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_batches_of_any_size_in_any_order():
+    # OpenCV 5.0 DNN crashed the process when a net was reused with a bigger batch than before (2, 5, 4, 4, 16)
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL)
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
+    one = rng.random((1, 3, 96, 96)).astype(np.float32)
+    ref = clf._forward(one)
+    for b in (2, 5, 4, 4, 16, 3, 24, 1, 9, 17):
+        x = rng.random((b, 3, 96, 96)).astype(np.float32)
+        x[0] = one[0]
+        out = clf._forward(x)
+        assert out.shape == (b, 3)
+        assert np.abs(out[0] - ref[0]).max() < 1e-4
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_plain_table_has_no_ends():
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL)
+    assert clf.ends(_table()) == []

+"""harness.decide: with default weights the equations make the same calls as the runner's rules."""
+
+import random
+
+
+
+from harness.decide import (Action, AttemptState, DoneWeights, FailWeights, Look, StageModel, action_value,
+                            attempt_fails, choose, look_failing, plan_timeouts, run_looks)
+
+CONFIRM, WINDOW = 2, 15.0
+
+
+def runner_done(looks):
+    """The done branch of Runner._watch, transcribed (frames always present, no immediate verdicts)."""
+    consec_done, last_done_t = 0, None
+    for lk in looks:
+        failing = lk.done is False or lk.failing
+        if lk.done is True and lk.same_frames:
+            continue
+        if lk.done is True:
+            consec_done += 1
+            recent = last_done_t is not None and lk.t - last_done_t <= WINDOW
+            last_done_t = lk.t
+            if consec_done >= CONFIRM or recent:
+                return lk.t
+        elif failing and last_done_t is not None and lk.t - last_done_t <= WINDOW:
+            pass
+        elif failing:
+            consec_done = 0
+        elif lk.done is not None or lk.monitor:
+            consec_done = 0
+    return None
+
+
+def random_looks(rng):
+    t, out, have_done = 0.0, [], False
+    for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 14)):
+        t += rng.choice([1.0, 3.0, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0, 20.0])
+        done = rng.choice([True, True, False, None])
+        out.append(Look(t, done, failing=rng.random() < 0.2, same_frames=have_done and done is True and rng.random() < 0.3,
+                        monitor=rng.random() < 0.8))
+        have_done = have_done or done is True
+    return out
+
+
+def test_default_done_weights_match_the_runner_on_random_sequences():
+    rng = random.Random(7)
+    for _ in range(5000):
+        looks = random_looks(rng)
+        assert run_looks(looks) == runner_done(looks), looks
+
+
+def test_done_cases_by_hand():
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, True)]) == 6                     # confirm 2
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, False), Look(9, True)]) == 9      # second done within the window
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(25, False), Look(28, True)]) is None  # outside it, the no resets
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, True, same_frames=True)]) is None  # same frames: one look, not two
+
+
+def test_fast_sensor_evidence_can_pass_a_stage_sooner_when_weighted():
+    looks = [Look(3, True, sensor_release=1.0)]
+    assert run_looks(looks) is None
+    assert run_looks(looks, DoneWeights(w_sensor_release=1.0)) == 3
+
+
+def runner_fails(a: AttemptState) -> bool:
+    """The fail rules of Runner._watch (stall-on-progress only checked past the settle gate, as here)."""
+    if a.consec_fail >= CONFIRM and (not a.monitor or a.elapsed >= 0.5 * a.timeout_s):
+        return True
+    if a.stall_s is not None and a.consec_stalled >= CONFIRM:
+        return True
+    if a.stall_s is not None and a.since_rise_s is not None and a.since_rise_s >= a.stall_s:
+        return True
+    return a.elapsed >= a.timeout_s
+
+
+def test_default_fail_weights_match_the_runner():
+    rng = random.Random(3)
+    for _ in range(20000):
+        timeout = rng.choice([10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 60.0])
+        stall = rng.choice([None, 20.0, 35.0])
+        a = AttemptState(elapsed=rng.uniform(0, 70), timeout_s=timeout, consec_fail=rng.randint(0, 3),
+                         consec_stalled=rng.randint(0, 3), since_rise_s=rng.choice([None, rng.uniform(0, 40)]),
+                         stall_s=stall, monitor=rng.random() < 0.8)
+        assert attempt_fails(a) == runner_fails(a), a
+
+
+def test_a_regression_makes_a_failing_look_not_an_instant_fail():
+    assert look_failing(None, False, progress=0.3, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=False)
+    assert not look_failing(None, False, progress=0.4, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=False)
+    assert not look_failing(None, False, progress=0.3, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=True)
+
+
+def test_sum_combination_lets_weak_signals_add_up():
+    a = AttemptState(elapsed=30, timeout_s=60, consec_fail=1, since_rise_s=10, stall_s=20)
+    assert not attempt_fails(a)                                  # every rule is half way
+    assert attempt_fails(a, FailWeights(combine="sum"))           # together they pass 1
+
+
+STAGES = [
+    StageModel("unplug", value=10, p_max=0.9, tau_c=25, t_min=5, gate=True),
+    StageModel("extra_cable", value=5, p_max=0.5, tau_c=15, t_min=4),
+    StageModel("tower", value=20, p_max=0.7, tau_c=40, t_min=8, gate=True),
+    StageModel("ball", value=5, p_max=0.6, tau_c=12, t_min=4),
+]
+
+
+def test_value_of_time_never_falls_as_time_grows():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    for i in range(len(STAGES)):
+        assert all(plan.W[i][L] <= plan.W[i][L + 1] + 1e-9 for L in range(300))
+    assert plan.value(0, 300) > plan.value(0, 60) > 0
+
+
+def test_optional_stage_gets_less_time_when_the_clock_is_short():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    assert plan.timeout(1, 60) <= plan.timeout(1, 250)
+    assert plan.timeout(1, 20) == 0 or plan.timeout(1, 20) < plan.timeout(1, 250)   # often skipped when short
+
+
+def test_gate_stage_is_never_skipped_while_it_can_still_pay():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    assert plan.timeout(0, 120) > 0 and plan.timeout(2, 100) > 0
+
+
+def test_action_choice_weighs_points_time_and_risk():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    keep = Action("keep_going", p=0.5, cost_s=10)
+    reset = Action("reset_then_retry", p=0.7, cost_s=25)
+    risky = Action("reach_across_tower", p=0.8, cost_s=8, risk=0.3)
+    assert choose(plan, 2, 200, [keep, reset, risky]).name != "reach_across_tower"
+    assert action_value(plan, 2, 5, reset) <= action_value(plan, 2, 5, keep)   # 5 s left: a 25 s reset can't pay
+
+
+def test_action_too_long_for_the_clock_is_worth_nothing():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 60)
+    assert action_value(plan, 3, 3, Action("x", p=1.0, cost_s=10)) == 0.0

+"""The decide shadow inside a real Runner: logs `decide` events, agrees with the runner on defaults, never acts."""
+
+import pytest
+from conftest import stage
+from test_v2 import Script, build, events, mv
+
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+
+
+def run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, stages, items, **top):
+    path = write_plan(stages, verifier="monitor", **top)
+    log = RunLog(tmp_path / "runs")
+    result = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script(items, repeat=False), log).run()
+    return result, events(log, "decide"), log
+
+
+def test_shadow_passes_on_the_same_look_as_the_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(None, progress=0.3, frames_sig="a"), mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="b"),
+             mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="b"),           # same frames: not a second look
+             mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="c")]
+    result, dec, log = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10)], items, confirm=2)
+    assert result.ok
+    assert [d["would_pass"] for d in dec] == [False, False, False, True]
+    assert [d["done_score"] for d in dec] == [0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 2.0]
+
+
+def test_stage_confirm_1_is_followed(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10, confirm=1)],
+                         [mv(None, frames_sig="a"), mv(True, frames_sig="b")], confirm=2)
+    assert result.ok and [d["would_pass"] for d in dec] == [False, True]
+
+
+def test_shadow_fails_on_the_same_look_as_the_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(None, "stalled"), mv(None, "ok"), mv(None, "stalled"), mv(None, "stalled")]
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10, stall_s=100, on_fail="skip")],
+                         items, confirm=2)
+    assert result.stages[0].reason.startswith("stalled")
+    assert [d["would_fail"] for d in dec] == [False, False, False, True]
+    assert dec[-1]["terms"]["stalled"] == 1.0
+
+
+def test_plan_weights_change_only_the_log(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(True, frames_sig="a"), mv(None, frames_sig="b"), mv(True, frames_sig="c"), mv(True, frames_sig="d")]
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10)], items, confirm=2,
+                         decide={"done": {"theta": 1.0}})
+    assert result.ok
+    assert dec[0]["would_pass"] is True        # the shadow (threshold 1) would have passed on the first done
+    assert len(dec) == 3                       # the runner still decided (second done within 15 s): the shadow did not act
+
+
+def test_unknown_weight_is_a_plan_error(write_plan):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")], decide={"done": {"w_monitr": 2}})
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown weight"):
+        load_plan(path)

+"""Hedged monitor calls, OpenRouter request translation, layered prompts, plan keys."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import time
+
+from conftest import stage
+
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.promptgen import Context, compose
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, to_openai_messages
+
+
+def test_openai_translation_keeps_images_and_system():
+    payload = {"system": "be a monitor", "max_tokens": 50, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": [
+        {"type": "text", "text": "is it done?"},
+        {"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": "image/jpeg", "data": "QUJD"}}]}]}
+    msgs = to_openai_messages(payload)
+    assert msgs[0] == {"role": "system", "content": "be a monitor"}
+    parts = msgs[1]["content"]
+    assert parts[0] == {"type": "text", "text": "is it done?"}
+    assert parts[1]["image_url"]["url"] == "data:image/jpeg;base64,QUJD"
+
+
+def test_layered_prompt_levels():
+    pre = "The strip has no power."
+    p = f"{pre} Pull the cable out."
+    ctx = Context(prefix=pre, holding={"right": "a cable", "left": "nothing"}, tower=["blue cube"], attempt=2,
+                  progress=0.4, last_failure="the gripper slipped off the plug")
+    assert compose(p, 0, ctx) == "Pull the cable out."
+    assert compose(p, 1, ctx) == p
+    l5 = compose(p, 5, ctx)
+    assert l5.startswith(pre) and l5.endswith("Pull the cable out.")
+    for bit in ("right gripper holds a cable", "blue cube", "try 2", "40%", "slipped", "8-ball"):
+        assert bit in l5
+    assert "left gripper" not in l5
+
+
+def test_plan_reads_new_keys(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")])
+    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    path.write_text(raw + "\nconfirm_gap_s: 3\nprompt_level: 4\nfast: {rate_hz: 25, mode: live}\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    assert plan.confirm_gap_s == 3.0 and plan.prompt_level == 4 and plan.fast["rate_hz"] == 25
+
+
+def test_hedged_monitor_takes_the_first_good_reply(write_plan):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")])
+    path.write_text(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\nclaude: {hedge: 3}\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    delays = iter([0.4, 0.05, 0.3])
+    reply = "<json>" + json.dumps({"stage_done": True, "confidence": 0.9, "progress": 0.9, "status": "ok", "reason": "fast one"}) + "</json>"
+
+    def transport(payload):
+        time.sleep(next(delays))
+        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": reply}]}
+    mv = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=transport)
+    t = time.monotonic()
+    v = mv._call({"_frames_sig": "x"}, 0)
+    assert v.done is True and time.monotonic() - t < 0.3 and mv.hedge_wins == [1]
+
+
+def test_reasoning_level_reaches_openrouter(monkeypatch):
+    from harness.verifiers import openrouter_request_body
+    payload = {"max_tokens": 10, "output_config": {"effort": "low"}, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "x"}]}
+    assert openrouter_request_body("m", payload)["reasoning"] == {"effort": "low"}
+    monkeypatch.setenv("HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING", "none")
+    assert openrouter_request_body("m", payload)["reasoning"] == {"enabled": False}
+
+
+def test_parser_accepts_fenced_and_bare_json():
+    from harness.verifiers import parse_monitor_reply
+    obj = '{"stage_done": true, "confidence": 0.9, "progress": 1, "status": "ok", "reason": "r", "guard": false}'
+    for text in (f"<json>{obj}</json>", f"thinking...\n```json\n{obj}\n```", f"Q1: yes. {obj}"):
+        v = parse_monitor_reply(text, 0)
+        assert v.done is True and v.guard is False, text

new file mode 100644

+"""final-harness: the pieces added when main, start-pose-live-guard and hybrid-harness were put together."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+from conftest import stage
+
+from harness import runner as runner_mod
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.runner import Runner
+
+
+class _Station:
+    dry_run = False
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.posts: list[str] = []
+
+    def reset(self) -> dict:
+        self.posts.append("/reset")
+        return {"state": "resetting"}
+
+    def home(self) -> dict:
+        self.posts.append("/home")
+        return {"state": "homing"}
+
+    def status(self) -> dict:
+        return {"state": "idle"}
+
+    def wait_for_state(self, states, timeout_s, poll_s=0.5) -> dict:
+        return {"state": "idle"}
+
+
+class _Guard:
+    def __init__(self, armed: bool) -> None:
+        self.armed, self.tripped = armed, None
+
+
+def _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, guard=None) -> tuple[Runner, _Station]:
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a")]))
+    st = _Station()
+    r = Runner(plan, st, None, {}, RunLog(tmp_path / "run"), sleep=lambda s: None)
+    r.live_guard = guard
+    r._t0 = r._clock()
+    return r, st
+
+
+def _events(r: Runner, kind: str) -> list[dict]:
+    lines = (r.log.path / "events.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+    return [e for e in map(json.loads, lines) if e.get("event") == kind or e.get("kind") == kind]
+
+
+def test_reset_is_skipped_while_the_live_guard_is_armed(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r, st = _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, _Guard(armed=True))
+    r._reset_and_wait("auto reset 1 after: SVD did not converge")
+    assert st.posts == []                       # /reset moves both arms and can't be cancelled: not while armed
+    assert _events(r, "reset_skipped")
+
+
+def test_reset_goes_through_the_motion_checks(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r, st = _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, _Guard(armed=False))
+    r._stop_event.set()                         # operator stop: nothing that moves an arm may follow
+    with pytest.raises(runner_mod._Stop):
+        r._reset_and_wait("reset_first")
+    assert st.posts == []
+
+
+def test_reset_is_sent_when_the_guard_is_disarmed(write_plan, tmp_path):

+def test_match_turns_the_operator_off(write_plan, monkeypatch):
+    seen = {}
+
+    def stop_before_running(*a, **k):
+        raise SystemExit(0)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RunLog", stop_before_running)
+    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.input", lambda *_: "yes")
+    orig = cli._parser
+
+    def parser():
+        p = orig()
+        parse = p.parse_args
+
+        def wrapped(argv=None):
+            ns = parse(argv)
+            seen["ns"] = ns
+            return ns
+        p.parse_args = wrapped
+        return p
+    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "_parser", parser)
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
+        cli.main([str(PLANS / "hill_climb_v4.yaml"), "--live", "--match"])
+    assert seen["ns"].no_operator is True

+"""The optimised detector must answer exactly as the one the match was tested with.
+
+tests/_detect_reference.py is harness/detect.py as of f756095, before the Apple Silicon work. On every recorded frame
+in tests/data/guard_frames the guard verdict, every box and every annotated JPEG must be identical; golden.json holds
+the verdicts the reference gave when the frames were added. The frames (8 Nov, all chosen with no people in view, the
+repo is public): real_* station wrist frames from the NUC's last-run_inputs, sim_* Dream YAM sim wrist frames, synth_*
+real frames with a white or black ball painted at the fingers (five are guard yeses), table_* overhead views. Set HARNESS_GUARD_FRAMES=<folder> to also compare on a folder of your own frames (searched recursively).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import os
+import random
+import sys
+from dataclasses import asdict
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from harness import detect
+from harness.detect import TableDetector
+
+ndi = pytest.importorskip("scipy.ndimage")
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+FRAMES = HERE / "data" / "guard_frames"
+CONFIG = HERE.parent / "plans" / "table_colors.yaml"
+
+
+def _reference():
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_detect_reference", HERE / "_detect_reference.py")
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+
+REF = _reference()
+
+
+def _frames() -> list[Path]:
+    files = sorted(FRAMES.glob("*.jpg"))
+    extra = os.environ.get("HARNESS_GUARD_FRAMES")
+    if extra:
+        files += sorted(Path(extra).rglob("*.jpg"))
+    return files
+
+
+def _role(path: Path) -> str:
+    return "wrist_l" if "wrist_l" in str(path) else "wrist_r"
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
+def dets():
+    return TableDetector.from_file(CONFIG), REF.TableDetector.from_file(CONFIG)
+
+
+def test_golden_guard_verdicts(dets):
+    new, _ = dets
+    golden = json.loads((FRAMES / "golden.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert len(golden) >= 20 and sum(v["guard"] is True for v in golden.values()) >= 3   # positives are in the set
+    for name, want in golden.items():
+        got = new.guard({want["role"]: (FRAMES / name).read_bytes()})
+        assert got == (want["guard"], want["why"]), name
+
+
+def test_guard_boxes_and_annotations_match_the_reference(dets):
+    new, ref = dets
+    files = _frames()
+    assert files
+    for f in files:
+        jpeg, role = f.read_bytes(), _role(f)
+        assert new.guard({role: jpeg}) == ref.guard({role: jpeg}), f
+        assert new.guard({"scene": jpeg, role: jpeg}) == ref.guard({"scene": jpeg, role: jpeg}), f
+        for overhead in (False, True):
+            got = [asdict(b) for b in detect.detect(jpeg, new.cfg, overhead=overhead)]
+            want = [asdict(b) for b in REF.detect(jpeg, ref.cfg, overhead=overhead)]
+            assert got == want, (f, overhead)
+    trio = {"scene": files[-1].read_bytes(), "wrist_l": files[0].read_bytes(), "wrist_r": files[1].read_bytes()}
+    assert new.annotate_frames(trio) == ref.annotate_frames(trio)
+
+
+def test_unreadable_and_non_wrist_frames_match(dets):
+    new, ref = dets
+    for frames in ({"wrist_r": b"not a jpeg"}, {"scene": (FRAMES / "golden.json").read_bytes()}, {}):
+        assert new.guard(frames) == ref.guard(frames)
+
+
+def _random_masks():
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
+    shapes = [(1, 1), (1, 9), (9, 1), (2, 2), (3, 5), (7, 7), (40, 64), (360, 640)]
+    for shape in shapes:
+        for density in (0.0, 0.05, 0.3, 0.6, 0.95, 1.0):
+            yield rng.random(shape) < density
+    # blobs with holes and blobs touching the edges
+    m = np.zeros((60, 80), bool)
+    m[5:25, 5:25] = True
+    m[10:15, 10:15] = False
+    m[40:60, 60:80] = True
+    m[0:10, 40:50] = True
+    m[48:52, 68:72] = False
+    yield m
+
+
+def test_close_and_fill_are_scipys():
+    for m in _random_masks():
+        want_c = ndi.binary_closing(m, iterations=2)
+        got_c = detect._close2(m)
+        assert np.array_equal(got_c, want_c), m.shape
+        assert np.array_equal(detect._fill_holes(want_c), ndi.binary_fill_holes(want_c)), m.shape
+        assert np.array_equal(detect._fill_holes(m), ndi.binary_fill_holes(m)), m.shape
+
+
+def test_in_range_is_the_int16_comparison():
+    rng = random.Random(5)
+    img = np.random.default_rng(5).integers(0, 256, (37, 53, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
+    planes = tuple(np.ascontiguousarray(img[..., c]) for c in range(3))
+    wide = img.astype(np.int16)
+    edges = [-5, 0, 1, 37, 128, 200, 254, 255, 256, 300]
+    for _ in range(400):
+        lo = tuple(rng.choice(edges + [rng.randint(0, 255)]) for _ in range(3))
+        hi = tuple(rng.choice(edges + [rng.randint(0, 255)]) for _ in range(3))
+        assert np.array_equal(detect._in_range(planes, lo, hi), REF._in_range(wide, lo, hi)), (lo, hi)
+
+
+def test_stitch_tile_cache_gives_the_same_grid():
+    from harness import verifiers
+
+    files = _frames()[:9]
+    imgs = [(f.name, f.read_bytes()) for f in files]
+    verifiers._TILES.clear()
+    cold = verifiers.stitch(imgs)
+    warm = verifiers.stitch(imgs)                    # every tile from the cache
+    verifiers._TILES.clear()
+    mixed_first = verifiers.stitch(imgs[:5])
+    mixed = verifiers.stitch(imgs)                   # half cached, half new
+    assert cold is not None and cold == warm == mixed and mixed_first != cold
+    assert len(verifiers._TILES) <= verifiers._TILE_CACHE

+def test_reset_first_then_auto_reset_after_svd(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    fake.reset_s = fake.home_s = 0.05
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=6.0)], reset_first=True)
+    hits = {"n": 0}
+
+    def svd(st, frames, elapsed):
+        hits["n"] += 1
+        if hits["n"] in (2, 6, 10):
+            with fake._lock:
+                fake.last_error = "LinAlgError: SVD did not converge"
+                fake.state = "error"
+        return mv(True, progress=1.0) if hits["n"] > 14 else mv(None)
+
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([svd]))
+    r.run()
+    rs = routes(fake)
+    assert rs[0] == "/reset" and rs[1] == "/start"              # grippers open before the first stage
+    assert len(events(r.log, "auto_reset")) == AUTO_RESET_MAX   # a third SVD restarts without a reset
+    assert rs.count("/reset") == 1 + AUTO_RESET_MAX

+                  '"note": "carry blocks high"}</json>')
+    op = Operator(plan, log, transport=t, lessons_path=lessons, print_fn=lambda s: None)
+    log.listeners.append(op.record)
+    notes = []
+
+    class V(Script):
+        operator_notes = notes
+
+    sv = V([lambda st, f, e: (op.submit("skip the next step and stop this one") if st.name == "a" and len(sv.calls) == 2
+                              else None) or mv(None)])
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv, log=log)
+    r.operator = op
+    res = r.run()
+    by = {s.name: s for s in res.stages}
+    assert by["a"].reason == "operator ended the stage" and by["b"].reason == "skipped by operator"
+    assert not any(c[0] == "b" for c in sv.calls) and any(c[0] == "c" for c in sv.calls)
+    assert notes == ["carry blocks high"]
+    assert "carry blocks high" in load_lessons(lessons)
+    kinds = [e["event"] for e in events(log)]
+    assert "operator_input" in kinds and "operator_reply" in kinds and "operator_action" in kinds
+    assert "skip the next step" in t.payloads[0]["messages"][0]["content"][-1]["text"]

+def _jpeg(color):
+    import io
+    from PIL import Image
+    out = io.BytesIO()
+    Image.new("RGB", (640, 360), color).save(out, "JPEG")
+    return out.getvalue()

+def test_stitched_history_and_two_calls_in_flight(write_plan):
+    import threading
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a", verifier="monitor")], claude={"stitch": True, "parallel": 2,
+                                                                        "history_frames": 4}))
+    gate, started = threading.Event(), []
+
+    def slow(payload):
+        started.append(payload)
+        gate.wait(5)
+        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": reply_text(progress=len(started) / 10)}]}
+
+    m = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=slow)
+    st = plan.stages[0]
+    for t in range(4):
+        m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg((t * 40, 0, 0)), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, float(t))
+    assert len(started) == 2                       # at most two in flight
+    gate.set()
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red"), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, 5.0)
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    last = started[-1]["messages"][0]["content"]
+    images = [c for c in last if c["type"] == "image"]
+    texts = [c["text"] for c in last if c["type"] == "text"]
+    assert len(images) == 3 and texts[0].startswith("Earlier frames in ONE image")   # grid + newest scene + wrist
+    v = m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red")}, 6.0)
+    assert v.latency_s is not None
+

+"""LiveKit observer: the parts that run without the livekit package (freshness gate, argument checks)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from harness import livekit_observer as lko
+
+
+class _Room:
+    def __init__(self, connected: bool = True) -> None:
+        self.connected = connected
+
+    def isconnected(self) -> bool:
+        return self.connected
+
+
+def _cameras(latest: dict, roles=("overhead", "wrist_l"), connected=True, reconnecting=False):
+    cams = object.__new__(lko.LiveKitCameras)      # skip __init__: it needs the livekit SDK
+    cams.room = _Room(connected)
+    cams._roles = {f"track_{r}": r for r in roles}
+    cams._latest = latest
+    cams._reconnecting = reconnecting
+    return cams
+
+
+def _frame(t: float) -> lko.ReceivedFrame:
+    return lko.ReceivedFrame(event=None, received_at=t)
+
+
+def test_sample_needs_every_view_fresh_and_new():
+    a, b = _frame(9.5), _frame(9.8)
+    cams = _cameras({"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b})
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b}
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {"overhead": a}) == {}          # one view unchanged since last sample
+    assert cams.sample(11.0, 1.0, {}) == {}                       # overhead is 1.5 s old
+    assert _cameras({"overhead": a}).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}  # wrist missing
+
+
+def test_sample_is_empty_while_disconnected_or_reconnecting():
+    latest = {"overhead": _frame(9.9), "wrist_l": _frame(9.9)}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), connected=False).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), reconnecting=True).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+
+
+def test_camera_names_match_station_aliases():
+    assert lko.camera_matches("video.top", "overhead")
+    assert lko.camera_matches("left", "wrist_l")
+    assert not lko.camera_matches("right", "wrist_l")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("argv, msg", [
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "ceiling=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--track", "wrist_l=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--plan", "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml"], "used together"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x"], "LIVEKIT_URL"),
+])
+def test_bad_arguments_are_refused(argv, msg, monkeypatch, capsys):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_URL", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_TOKEN", raising=False)
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
+        lko.main(argv)
+    assert exc.value.code == 2
+    assert msg in capsys.readouterr().err
+
+
+def test_seconds_must_be_positive():
+    import argparse
+    with pytest.raises(argparse.ArgumentTypeError):
+        lko.positive_seconds("0")
+    assert lko.positive_seconds("2.5") == 2.5

+"""plan.start_pose (home before a stage when the arms start far from every recorded start) and the live-frame guard."""
+
+import json
+import threading
+
+import pytest
+
+from conftest import stage, wait_until
+from test_v2 import Script, build, events, mv, routes
+from harness.live_guard import LiveFrames, LiveGuard
+from harness.plan import parse_plan
+from harness.start_pose import StartPoses, joints_from_arms, joints_from_observation
+
+HOME = [0.0] * 6
+RAISED = [0.3, -1.7, 0.75, 0.1, 0.05, -0.1]
+
+
+def _ref(**by_prompt):
+    return StartPoses({p: [{"left": l, "right": r, "episode": f"ep{i}"} for i, (l, r) in enumerate(starts)]
+                       for p, starts in by_prompt.items()})
+
+
+# -- start pose: the check --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_check_statuses():
+    ref = _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    near = {"left": HOME, "right": [0.1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]}
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", near, 0.35).status == "ok"
+    far = ref.check("do unplug", {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}, 0.35)
+    assert far.status == "far" and far.distance["right"] == pytest.approx(1.7) and far.nearest == "ep0"
+    assert ref.check("other prompt", near, 0.35).status == "no_reference"
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", None, 0.35).status == "no_state"
+
+
+def test_nearest_start_wins():
+    ref = _ref(p=[(HOME, HOME), (HOME, RAISED)])
+    chk = ref.check("p", {"left": HOME, "right": [0.25, -1.6, 0.7, 0.1, 0.0, -0.1]}, 0.35)
+    assert chk.status == "ok" and chk.nearest == "ep1"
+
+
+def test_joint_sources():
+    assert joints_from_arms({"left": {"gripper": 1.0}, "right": {"gripper": 1.0}}) is None   # old add-on
+    assert joints_from_arms({"right": {"positions_rad": RAISED}}) == {"right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"state.left_arm": HOME, "state.right_arm": RAISED, "prompt": "x"}) == \
+        {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"prompt": "x"}) is None
+
+
+# -- start pose: the plan ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _plan(**sp):
+    return parse_plan({"task": "t", "start_pose": {"reference": "r.json", **sp},
+                       "stages": [{"name": "unplug_cable", "prompt": "u", "success": "u?"}]})
+
+
+def test_start_pose_parses():
+    sp = _plan(home_before=["unplug"], tolerance_rad=0.2).start_pose
+    assert sp.homes("unplug_cable") and sp.tolerance_rad == 0.2 and sp.max_homes == 1 and sp.home_pose["left"] == HOME
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("sp, match", [
+    ({"home_before": ["stack"]}, "matches no stage"),
+    ({"home_pose": {"left": HOME}}, "home_pose"),
+    ({"tolerance_rad": 0}, "tolerance_rad"),
+    ({"homes": 2}, "unknown key"),
+])
+def test_bad_start_pose_rejected(sp, match):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=match):
+        _plan(**sp)
+

+# -- start pose: the runner -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, *, right, home_before=("unplug_cable",), refs=None, verdicts=None,
+                 max_attempts=2):
+    path = write_plan([stage("unplug_cable", prompt="do unplug", max_attempts=max_attempts, timeout_s=0.6)],
+                      start_pose={"reference": "unused.json", "home_before": list(home_before)}, time_budget_s=300)
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script(verdicts or [mv(True)]))
+    runner.start_poses = refs or _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": right}}
+    return runner
+
+
+def test_far_start_homes_before_the_first_prompt(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.index("/home") < r.index("/start")
+    ev = events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]
+    assert ev["status"] == "far" and ev["source"] == "arms" and ev["why"] == "stage start"
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")[0]["done"] is True
+
+
+def test_near_start_does_not_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=HOME)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "ok"
+
+
+def test_other_stages_only_log(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, home_before=())
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake) and events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "far"
+
+
+def test_no_home_when_home_is_not_a_recorded_start(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    refs = _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, [1.5] * 6)]})
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, refs=refs)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")[0]["done"] is False

+def test_max_homes_per_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED, verdicts=[mv(False, "failing")], max_attempts=3)
+    runner.run()
+    assert routes(fake).count("/home") == 1          # stage start homed; the retries only logged
+    homes = events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")
+    assert homes[0]["done"] is True and all(h["done"] is False for h in homes[1:]) and len(homes) >= 2
+

+def test_retry_after_far_start_homes_then_starts_fresh(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    arms = {"right": HOME, "failed": False}
+    verdict = Script([lambda st, f, e: mv(True) if arms["failed"] else mv(False, "failing")])
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=HOME, verdicts=None)
+    runner.verifiers = {k: verdict for k in runner.verifiers}
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": arms["right"]}}
+    orig = runner._attempt
+
+    def attempt(*a, **k):
+        out = orig(*a, **k)
+        arms["right"], arms["failed"] = RAISED, True   # the failed attempt left the arm up over the hub
+        return out
+    runner._attempt = attempt
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.count("/home") == 1 and r.count("/start") == 2 and r.index("/home") < len(r) - 1
+    assert [e["mode"] for e in events(runner.log, "retry")] == ["home_then_retry"]
+

+def test_no_reference_file_means_no_checks(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    runner.start_poses = None
+    assert runner.run().ok and not events(runner.log, "start_pose")

+def test_reference_tool(tmp_path):
+    pa = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow")
+    pq = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow.parquet")
+    from tools.start_poses import main as build_ref
+    ds = tmp_path / "set"
+    (ds / "meta").mkdir(parents=True)
+    (ds / "data" / "chunk-000").mkdir(parents=True)
+    names = [f"left_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + ["left_gripper"] + [f"right_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + \
+        ["right_gripper"]
+    (ds / "meta" / "info.json").write_text(json.dumps({"features": {"observation.state": {"names": names}}}))
+    (ds / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").write_text('{"task_index": 0, "task": "a"}\n{"task_index": 1, "task": "b"}\n')
+    rows = [[0.0] * 7 + [0.1] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.2] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.9] * 7]
+    pq.write_table(pa.table({"observation.state": rows, "task_index": [0, 0, 1]}),
+                   ds / "data" / "chunk-000" / "episode_000000.parquet")
+    out = tmp_path / "ref.json"
+    assert build_ref([str(out), str(ds)]) == 0
+    ref = StartPoses.load(out)
+    assert ref.by_prompt["a"][0]["right"] == [0.1] * 6 and ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["right"] == [0.9] * 6
+    assert ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["episode"] == "set/episode_000000/seg1"

+# -- live guard -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+class _Feed:
+    """Distinct frames each grab unless frozen."""
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.n, self.frozen = 0, False
+
+    def __call__(self):
+        if not self.frozen:
+            self.n += 1
+        return {"wrist_r": b"jpeg%d" % self.n}
+
+
+def _guard(answers, confirm=3):
+    stops, log = [], []
+    seq = list(answers)
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: seq.pop(0) if seq else (False, "no"), lambda: stops.append(1),
+                  confirm_frames=confirm, log=lambda name, **k: log.append((name, k)))
+    return g, stops, log
+
+
+def test_live_guard_needs_confirm_distinct_yes_in_a_row():
+    g, stops, log = _guard([(True, "y"), (False, "n"), (True, "y"), (None, "?"), (True, "y"), (True, "y")])
+    g.step()                                   # disarmed: nothing is looked at
+    assert g.ticks == 0
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped == "y" and stops == [1]
+    assert [k["count"] for n, k in log if n == "guard_yes"] == [1, 1, 2, 3]
+    g.step()
+    assert stops == [1]                        # stops once
+
+
+def test_live_guard_frozen_frames_count_once():
+    g, stops, _ = _guard([(True, "y")] * 5)
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.source.frozen = True
+    for _ in range(5):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops
+
+
+def test_live_guard_answer_time_and_disarm():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 100.0
+    t[0] = 105.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+    g.disarm()
+    t[0] = 110.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+
+
+def test_live_frames_probe_finds_no_route(fake):
+    lf = LiveFrames(fake.api_url, ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"))
+    assert lf.probe() == [] and "404" in lf.last_error
+    assert lf.roles == ["wrist_l", "wrist_r"]          # nothing dropped: a camera that comes back is used again
+

+# -- Codex review findings ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_yes_counts_per_camera_not_across_cameras():
+    n = {"l": 0}
+
+    def feed():
+        n["l"] += 1
+        return {"wrist_l": b"moving%d" % n["l"], "wrist_r": b"frozen-yes"}
+    stops = []
+    g = LiveGuard(feed, lambda f: (True, "y") if "wrist_r" in f else (False, "n"), lambda: stops.append(1))
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops            # one frozen yes frame is one look
+
+
+def test_frozen_frames_do_not_refresh_the_blind_clock():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(lambda: {"wrist_r": b"same"}, lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    t[0] = 101.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0
+    t[0] = 130.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0                   # max_blind_s will see the camera as blind
+

+def test_a_judgment_from_before_a_rearm_is_dropped():
+    g = None
+    stops = []
+
+    def judge(f):
+        g.disarm()
+        g.arm("ball_2")                               # the runner moved on while this frame was being judged
+        return True, "y"
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), judge, lambda: stops.append(1), confirm_frames=1)
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops
+

+def test_no_motion_command_after_a_trip(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: None, confirm_frames=1)
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert g.tripped
+    before = routes(fake)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._motion(runner.station.start, "do ball_1", None)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._issue("do ball_1", "stage")
+    assert routes(fake) == before                     # nothing was sent

+def test_operator_stop_during_the_stop_wait_means_no_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Stop
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    runner._t0 = runner._clock()
+    stage0 = runner.plan.stages[0]
+    runner._issue("do unplug", "stage", force_start=True)          # running, so the gate must /stop first
+    orig_stop = runner.station.stop
+    runner.station.stop = lambda: (runner.request_stop(), orig_stop())[1]
+    with pytest.raises(_Stop):
+        runner._start_pose_gate(stage0, "do unplug", "retry")
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)

+def test_a_trip_ends_a_wait_for_running_at_once(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    import time as _t
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner.live_guard = type("G", (), {"tripped": "white ball", "last_answer_t": None})()
+    t0 = _t.monotonic()
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard, match="while waiting for running"):
+        runner._wait({"running"}, 30.0, "running")       # the fake is idle: without the trip this waits 30 s
+    assert _t.monotonic() - t0 < 2.0
+

+def test_live_guard_trip_ends_the_match_mid_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("stack", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip"), stage("ball_1", timeout_s=5.0, on_fail="skip"),
+                       stage("ball_2", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip")],
+                      verifier="monitor", guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"]})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None, guard=False)]))
+    armed = threading.Event()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "white ball between the fingers"), runner.station.stop, rate_hz=50,
+                  confirm_frames=3, log=runner.log.event)
+    orig_arm = g.arm
+    g.arm = lambda s: (orig_arm(s), armed.set())
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.start()
+    try:
+        result = runner.run()
+    finally:
+        g.close()
+    assert armed.is_set()
+    assert not result.ok and result.guard_tripped and "live frames" in result.reason
+    assert [r.name for r in result.stages] == ["stack"]
+    trip = [e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_tripped") if e.get("source") == "live"]
+    assert trip and trip[0]["stage"] == "ball_1" and trip[0]["stop"] == "sent"
+    assert routes(fake).count("/stop") >= 1
+    assert not wait_until(lambda: g.ticks > 10_000, timeout=0.05)   # thread is idle after the trip
+
+
+# -- second Codex review -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_trip_during_a_motion_command_stops_again(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner._t0 = runner._clock()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: None, confirm_frames=1)
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+
+    def slow_start(prompt, checkpoint):
+        g.step()                                       # the guard trips while this POST is in flight
+        return runner.station.start(prompt, checkpoint)
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._motion(slow_start, "do ball_1", None)
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r[-2:] == ["/start", "/stop"]               # the start that overtook the trip is stopped
+

+def test_guard_trip_does_not_wait_for_the_runner():
+    stops = []
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "y"), lambda: stops.append(1), confirm_frames=1)
+    assert not hasattr(g, "command_lock")
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert stops == [1]

+def test_per_poll_guard_counts_new_frames_only(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("ball_1", timeout_s=1.5, on_fail="skip")], verifier="monitor",
+                      guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"], "confirm": 2})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    runner.frames.latest = lambda: {"wrist_r": b"the-same-cached-trip"}
+    runner.fast_guard = lambda f: (True, "white ball")
+    result = runner.run()
+    assert not result.guard_tripped                    # one frame, judged once: never two yes answers
+    assert len([e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_yes") if e.get("source") == "local"]) == 1
+

+# -- third Codex review ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_no_home_while_the_live_guard_is_armed(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    from harness.runner import _Fail
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))

+def test_per_poll_guard_ignores_a_frozen_wrist_when_the_scene_changes(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("ball_1", timeout_s=1.5, on_fail="skip")], verifier="monitor",

+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    n = {"i": 0}
+
+    def latest():
+        n["i"] += 1
+        return {"scene": b"scene%d" % n["i"], "wrist_r": b"frozen-wrist"}
+    runner.frames.latest = latest
+    runner.fast_guard = lambda f: (True, "white ball") if "wrist_r" in f else (False, "none")
+    result = runner.run()
+    assert not result.guard_tripped
+    assert len([e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_yes") if e.get("source") == "local"]) == 1

+def test_a_dead_camera_does_not_slow_the_live_one():
+    import time as _t
+    lf = LiveFrames("http://127.0.0.1:9", ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"))
+    lf._one = lambda role: (_t.sleep(1.0), None)[1] if role == "wrist_l" else (_t.sleep(0.01), b"jpeg")[1]
+    t0 = _t.monotonic()
+    got = [lf.grab(wait_s=0.05) for _ in range(5)]
+    took = _t.monotonic() - t0
+    assert all(g == {"wrist_r": b"jpeg"} for g in got)  # the live camera every tick
+    # the dead one (1 s per try) never held a tick up: ideal 5 x 50 ms; one blocked tick would make it >= 1 s.
+    # 0.9 s, not 0.5: a macOS CI runner's timed waits overshoot ~2x (0.508 s on macos-15, 8 Nov).
+    assert took < 0.9

+"""The matrix state-space model must make the runner's decisions (decide.py is the reference for the done rule)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import random
+import time
+
+import numpy as np
+
+from harness import decide
+from harness.hedge import first_of
+from harness.statespace import Observer, StateSpace, Weights
+
+
+def _looks(rng: random.Random, n: int = 40):
+    t, out = 0.0, []
+    for _ in range(n):
+        t += rng.choice([1.0, 2.5, 3.0, 5.0, 9.0])
+        r = rng.random()
+        done = True if r < 0.35 else (False if r < 0.55 else None)
+        failing = done is not True and rng.random() < 0.3
+        same = done is True and rng.random() < 0.15
+        out.append(decide.Look(t, done, failing=failing, same_frames=same, monitor=True))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ss_pass_time(looks, w: Weights) -> float | None:
+    ss = StateSpace(w)
+    ss.start(timeout_s=1e9, stall_s=None)
+    prev = 0.0
+    for lk in looks:
+        u = ss.u(dt=lk.t - prev, v_new=1, v_done=lk.done is True, v_same=lk.same_frames,
+                 v_failing=(lk.done is False) or lk.failing)
+        prev = lk.t
+        if ss.step(u)["pass"][0]:
+            return lk.t
+    return None
+
+
+def test_matches_reference_done_rule_on_random_runs():
+    rng = random.Random(7)
+    for _ in range(3000):
+        looks = _looks(rng)
+        ref = decide.run_looks(looks)
+        got = _ss_pass_time(looks, Weights(min_gap_s=0.0))
+        assert got == ref, (looks, ref, got)
+
+
+def test_min_gap_merges_close_dones():
+    w = Weights(min_gap_s=3.0)
+    looks = [decide.Look(10.0, True), decide.Look(11.0, True), decide.Look(14.5, True)]
+    assert _ss_pass_time(looks, w) == 14.5          # 11.0 is too close to 10.0 to count
+    assert _ss_pass_time(looks, Weights(min_gap_s=0.0)) == 11.0
+
+
+def test_vectorised_over_weight_sets():
+    ws = [Weights(confirm=c, min_gap_s=0.0, window_s=0.0) for c in (1, 2, 3)]
+    ss = StateSpace(ws)
+    ss.start(1e9, None)
+    passes = []
+    for k in range(3):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=5, v_new=1, v_done=1))
+        passes.append(d["pass"].copy())
+    assert passes[0].tolist() == [True, False, False]
+    assert passes[1].tolist() == [True, True, False]
+    assert passes[2].tolist() == [True, True, True]
+
+
+def test_guards_and_fail_rule():
+    ss = StateSpace(Weights())
+    ss.start(timeout_s=40, stall_s=None)
+    for _ in range(2):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=3, l_guard_yes=1))
+    assert not d["guard"][0]
+    d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=0.1, l_guard_yes=1))
+    assert d["guard"][0]                             # 3 live frames in a row
+    ss.start(timeout_s=40, stall_s=None)
+    for _ in range(2):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=5, v_new=1, v_failing=1))
+    assert not d["fail"][0]                          # only 10 s of a 40 s stage: too early
+    d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=15, v_new=1, v_failing=1))
+    assert d["fail"][0]
+
+
+def test_observer_blends_fast_and_slow():
+    ob = Observer(Weights(l_fast=0.25, l_slow=0.8))
+    ob.reset()
+    for _ in range(4):
+        ob.fast({"at_goal": 1.0})
+    fast_only = ob.z[2]
+    ob.slow({"at_goal": 0.0})                        # Claude says no: it wins most of the way
+    assert fast_only > 0.6 and ob.z[2] < 0.3
+
+
+def test_tick_is_fast():
+    ss = StateSpace([Weights() for _ in range(1000)])
+    ss.start(60, 20)
+    u = ss.u(dt=0.05, v_new=1, v_done=1)
+    t = time.perf_counter()
+    for _ in range(200):
+        ss.step(u)
+    per_tick_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t) / 200 * 1000
+    assert per_tick_ms < 5.0                         # 1,000 weight sets per tick, well inside a 50 ms (20 Hz) budget
+
+
+def test_first_of_takes_the_fastest_good_answer():
+    delays = iter([0.3, 0.05, 0.2])
+
+    def call():
+        d = next(delays)
+        time.sleep(d)
+        return d
+    res = first_of(call, 3)
+    assert res.value == 0.05 and res.latency_s < 0.2
+    bad = first_of(lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(RuntimeError("x")), 2, timeout_s=1)
+    assert res.winner is not None and bad.winner is None and bad.errors
+    assert np.isfinite(res.latency_s)

diff --git a/tools/bench_judges.py b/tools/bench_judges.py
