+"""Label Dream's own record of a harness run for fine-tuning.
+
+Dream keeps each run's record (the station's archiver copies it to ~/rollouts/zeroshot_<time>/): last-run.csv (20 Hz
+joints and commands) and last-run_inputs/inputs.jsonl (every model input: frames, state and the prompt it was given).
+The harness knows what those seconds were for. This joins the two:
+
+    python tools/label_rollout.py runs/<stamp> <rollout dir or its inputs.jsonl>
+
+and writes <rollout>/harness_labels.jsonl, one line per model input, plus harness_segments.json:
+  stage, attempt     the harness stage and attempt the input belongs to
+  prompt_sent        what Dream was told (stage prompt, a monitor rewrite, a recovery, an operator prompt)
+  source             stage | rewrite | recovery | operator | tower
+  label              the clean task label for training: the stage's own prompt (rewrites map back to it)
+  outcome            ok | failed for the attempt the input is in; usable = ok and not a recovery
+
+The clocks differ (Dream's t_s is monotonic, the harness logs epoch time). They are aligned on the first /start:
+its harness time is the first model input's time, to about a second.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import pathlib
+import sys
+
+
+def _load(path: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict]:
+    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines() if line.strip()]
+
+
+def _source(why: str) -> str:
+    why = why or ""
+    if why.startswith("monitor next_prompt"):
+        return "rewrite"
+    if why.startswith("recovery"):
+        return "recovery"
+    if why == "operator":
+        return "operator"
+    return "stage"
+
+
+def label(run: pathlib.Path, rollout: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
+    inputs_path = rollout if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout / "last-run_inputs" / "inputs.jsonl"
+    inputs = [r for r in _load(inputs_path) if r.get("kind") == "inputs"]
+    events = _load(run / "events.jsonl")
+    start = next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "start")
+    offset = start["t"] - inputs[0]["t_s"]   # harness epoch = Dream t_s + offset
+
+    # Spans: [t, stage, attempt, prompt, source, label], each running until the next prompt change.
+    spans, stage, attempt, base = [], None, 1, ""
+    outcomes: dict[tuple[str, int], str] = {}
+    for e in events:
+        ev = e["event"]
+        if ev == "stage_start":
+            stage, attempt, base = e["stage"], 1, e.get("prompt") or ""
+        elif ev == "tower_prompt" and e.get("next"):
+            base = e.get("prompt") or base
+        elif ev == "attempt_end":   # `retry` is logged after the retry's prompt, so count from here
+            outcomes[(e["stage"], e["attempt"])] = "ok" if e.get("ok") else "failed"
+            attempt = e["attempt"] + 1
+        elif ev == "prompt_change" and stage is not None:
+            src = _source(e.get("why"))
+            spans.append({"t": e["t"], "stage": stage, "attempt": attempt, "prompt_sent": e.get("prompt") or "",
+                          "source": src, "label": base if src in ("stage", "rewrite") else e.get("prompt") or ""})
+    rows = []
+    for r in inputs:
+        t = r["t_s"] + offset
+        span = next((s for s in reversed(spans) if s["t"] <= t + 0.5), None)
+        if span is None:
+            continue
+        outcome = outcomes.get((span["stage"], span["attempt"]), "unknown")
+        rows.append({"t_s": r["t_s"], **{k: span[k] for k in ("stage", "attempt", "prompt_sent", "source", "label")},
+                     "prompt_seen": (r.get("state") or {}).get("prompt"), "outcome": outcome,
+                     "usable": outcome == "ok" and span["source"] != "recovery"})
+    segs: list[dict] = []
+    for row in rows:
+        key = (row["stage"], row["attempt"])
+        if segs and (segs[-1]["stage"], segs[-1]["attempt"]) == key:
+            segs[-1]["t_end"] = row["t_s"]
+            segs[-1]["inputs"] += 1
+        else:
+            segs.append({"stage": row["stage"], "attempt": row["attempt"], "t_start": row["t_s"], "t_end": row["t_s"],
+                         "inputs": 1, "label": row["label"], "outcome": row["outcome"]})
+    return rows, segs
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    run, rollout = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
+    rows, segs = label(run, rollout)
+    out = rollout.parent if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout
+    (out / "harness_labels.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))
+    (out / "harness_segments.json").write_text(json.dumps(segs, indent=2))
+    mismatch = sum(1 for r in rows if r["prompt_seen"] and r["prompt_seen"] != r["prompt_sent"])
+    for s in segs:
+        print(f"{s['outcome']:<7} {s['stage']:<18} a{s['attempt']}  {s['t_end'] - s['t_start']:5.0f} s  "
+              f"{s['inputs']:3d} inputs  {s['label'][:70]}")
+    print(f"{len(rows)} inputs labelled, {sum(r['usable'] for r in rows)} usable; prompt mismatches "
+          f"(alignment check, want ~0): {mismatch}  -> {out}/harness_labels.jsonl")
diff --git a/tools/mac_bench.py b/tools/mac_bench.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b0ba07d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/mac_bench.py
@@ -0,0 +1,354 @@
+"""Offline benchmark of the harness's live path, for the match laptop (Apple Silicon MacBook Air) and any other box.
+
+No station, no network, no paid API call: it replays recorded wrist frames (tests/data/guard_frames by default) through
+the same code the match runs, and measures per-iteration latency (p50 / p95 / max, ms) and CPU (% of one core, from
+process CPU time over wall time) for:
+
+  guard          TableDetector.guard on one wrist JPEG (the live guard's and fast loop's per-frame work)
+  guard_ref      the same on the frozen pre-optimisation detector (tests/_detect_reference.py): before/after on one box
+  fastloop       FastLoop.step with two new wrist frames a tick (detector included), as at 20 Hz in a match
+  statespace     Observer.fast + StateSpace.step alone (the decision model, no perception)
+  jpeg_decode    decode one 640 px frame to RGB;  jpeg_encode: encode it at q85
+  stitch         verifiers.stitch on 9 history frames, one of them new (the monitor's grid image, one per check)
+  annotate       TableDetector.annotate_frames on scene + both wrists (boxes drawn on the monitor's frames)
+  monitor_build  MonitorVerifier._build: annotate + stitch + base64 + stage text, i.e. one whole monitor request
+  prompt         promptgen.compose at level 5 (the longest layered prompt)
+
+--sustained S runs the match load for S seconds (live guard 10 Hz + fast loop 20 Hz on both wrists, in their own
+threads, a monitor request every 3 s) and prints a row per 30 s window: guard p50/p95, fast-loop tick p95, overruns
+(ticks longer than their period) and CPU %. On a fanless Air, a p95 that climbs window after window is thermal
+throttling. --sustained-mode max runs the guard flat out on --threads threads instead (worst-case heat).
+
+    python tools/mac_bench.py                              # component table, ~30 s
+    python tools/mac_bench.py --sustained 300              # + 5 minutes of match load
+    python tools/mac_bench.py --sustained 300 --sustained-mode max --threads 4
+Results go to bench_results/mac_bench_<host>_<time>.json (--out to change).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import importlib.util
+import io
+import json
+import os
+import platform
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+
+from harness.perf import cap_threads  # noqa: E402
+
+cap_threads()
+
+import numpy as np  # noqa: E402
+from PIL import Image  # noqa: E402
+
+from harness.detect import TableDetector  # noqa: E402
+from harness.fastloop import FastLoop  # noqa: E402
+from harness.plan import load_plan  # noqa: E402
+from harness.promptgen import Context, compose  # noqa: E402
+from harness.statespace import Observer, StateSpace, Weights  # noqa: E402
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, stitch  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def _reference_detector(cfg_path: Path):
+    ref = ROOT / "tests" / "_detect_reference.py"
+    if not ref.exists():
+        return None
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_detect_reference", ref)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod      # dataclasses look their module up by name
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod.TableDetector.from_file(cfg_path)
+
+
+def _stats(ms: list[float], cpu_s: float, wall_s: float) -> dict[str, float]:
+    a = np.sort(np.asarray(ms))
+    return {"n": int(a.size), "p50_ms": float(np.percentile(a, 50)), "p95_ms": float(np.percentile(a, 95)),
+            "max_ms": float(a[-1]), "mean_ms": float(a.mean()), "cpu_pct": 100.0 * cpu_s / max(wall_s, 1e-9)}
+
+
+def bench(fn, *, seconds: float, min_iters: int = 20) -> dict[str, float]:
+    """Runs fn() back to back for `seconds` (at least min_iters times) after a short warm-up."""
+    for _ in range(3):
+        fn()
+    ms: list[float] = []
+    c0, w0 = time.process_time(), time.perf_counter()
+    while len(ms) < min_iters or time.perf_counter() - w0 < seconds:
+        t = time.perf_counter()
+        fn()
+        ms.append(1000 * (time.perf_counter() - t))
+    return _stats(ms, time.process_time() - c0, time.perf_counter() - w0)
+
+
+class Cycle:
+    """Endless supply of recorded frames. Each one is made unique (a counter after the JPEG's end marker, which
+    decoders ignore), as a live camera's frames are, so no digest-keyed cache can reuse an earlier result."""
+
+    def __init__(self, frames: list[bytes]):
+        self.frames, self.i = frames, 0
+
+    def next(self) -> bytes:
+        j = self.frames[self.i % len(self.frames)]
+        self.i += 1
+        return j + self.i.to_bytes(8, "little")
+
+
+def _load_frames(folder: Path) -> tuple[list[bytes], list[bytes]]:
+    files = sorted(folder.glob("*.jpg"))
+    if not files:
+        raise SystemExit(f"no .jpg frames in {folder}")
+    wrists = [f.read_bytes() for f in files if "wrist" in f.name] or [f.read_bytes() for f in files]
+    # overhead-like frames for the monitor's scene view; a top-down one first if there is one
+    others = [f.read_bytes() for f in sorted((f for f in files if "wrist" not in f.name),
+                                             key=lambda f: "topdown" not in f.name)] or wrists
+    return wrists, others
+
+
+def components(args, det: TableDetector, ref, wrists: list[bytes], others: list[bytes]) -> dict[str, dict]:
+    s = args.seconds
+    out: dict[str, dict] = {}
+    cyc = Cycle(wrists)
+    out["guard"] = bench(lambda: det.guard({"wrist_r": cyc.next()}), seconds=s)
+    if ref is not None:
+        cyc_r = Cycle(wrists)
+        out["guard_ref"] = bench(lambda: ref.guard({"wrist_r": cyc_r.next()}), seconds=s)
+
+    cyc_f = Cycle(wrists)
+    fl = FastLoop(frames=lambda: {"wrist_l": cyc_f.next(), "wrist_r": cyc_f.next()}, guard_judge=det.guard,
+                  grippers=lambda: {"left": {"opening": 0.5}, "right": {"opening": 0.1}})
+    fl.attempt_start("pick_ball", 60.0, 20.0, guarded=True)
+    out["fastloop"] = bench(fl.step, seconds=s)
+
+    ss, ob = StateSpace(Weights()), Observer(Weights())
+    ss.start(60.0, 20.0)
+    ob.reset()
+    u = ss.u(dt=0.05, l_guard_no=1.0)
+
+    def ss_step():
+        ob.fast({"illegal_grip": 0.0, "holding": 1.0})
+        ss.step(u, ob.z)
+    out["statespace"] = bench(ss_step, seconds=s, min_iters=2000)
+
+    jpeg = wrists[0]
+    out["jpeg_decode"] = bench(lambda: Image.open(io.BytesIO(jpeg)).convert("RGB"), seconds=s)
+    rgb = Image.open(io.BytesIO(jpeg)).convert("RGB")
+
+    def enc():
+        b = io.BytesIO()
+        rgb.save(b, "JPEG", quality=85)
+    out["jpeg_encode"] = bench(enc, seconds=s)
+    # A check's grid: the stage-start frame plus a sliding history, so each check brings one new frame
+    cyc_s = Cycle(wrists)
+    hist = [("stage start", cyc_s.next())] + [(f"t={2 * i} s", cyc_s.next()) for i in range(8)]
+
+    def stitch_check():
+        del hist[1]
+        hist.append(("newest history", cyc_s.next()))
+        stitch(hist)
+    out["stitch"] = bench(stitch_check, seconds=s)
+    scene = others[0]
+    trio = {"scene": scene, "wrist_l": wrists[0], "wrist_r": wrists[1 % len(wrists)]}
+    out["annotate"] = bench(lambda: det.annotate_frames(trio), seconds=s)
+
+    plan = load_plan(ROOT / "plans" / "hill_climb_v4.yaml")
+    mv = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=lambda payload: {"content": []})
+    mv.annotator, mv.legend = det.annotate_frames, det.legend()
+    stage = next(st for st in plan.stages if plan.guard is not None and plan.guard.covers(st.name))
+    cyc_m = Cycle(wrists)
+    t_el = [0.0]
+
+    def build():
+        t_el[0] += 3.0
+        payload = mv._build(stage, {"scene": scene, "wrist_l": cyc_m.next(), "wrist_r": cyc_m.next()}, t_el[0])
+        json.dumps({k: v for k, v in (payload or {}).items() if not k.startswith("_")})
+    out["monitor_build"] = bench(build, seconds=s)
+
+    ctx = Context(prefix="The strip has no power.", holding={"right": "a cable", "left": "nothing"},
+                  tower=["blue cube", "pink cube"], attempt=2, progress=0.4, last_failure="the gripper slipped")
+    p = "The strip has no power. Pull the black cable out of the hub with the right arm."
+    out["prompt"] = bench(lambda: compose(p, 5, ctx), seconds=s, min_iters=2000)
+    return out
+
+
+def sustained(args, det: TableDetector, wrists: list[bytes], others: list[bytes]) -> list[dict]:
+    """Match load (or flat out) for args.sustained seconds; one row per args.window seconds."""
+    stop = threading.Event()
+    lock = threading.Lock()
+    rec: dict[str, list[float]] = {"guard": [], "tick": []}
+    over = {"n": 0}
+
+    def timed_guard(frames):
+        t = time.perf_counter()
+        r = det.guard(frames)
+        with lock:
+            rec["guard"].append(1000 * (time.perf_counter() - t))
+        return r
+
+    threads: list[threading.Thread] = []
+    if args.sustained_mode == "match":
+        cyc_g, cyc_f = Cycle(wrists), Cycle(wrists[::-1])
+
+        def periodic(period: float, body, record_tick: bool = False):
+            def run():
+                while not stop.is_set():
+                    t = time.perf_counter()
+                    body()
+                    took = time.perf_counter() - t
+                    with lock:
+                        if record_tick:
+                            rec["tick"].append(1000 * took)
+                        if took > period:
+                            over["n"] += 1
+                    stop.wait(max(0.0, period - took))
+            return run
+        fl = FastLoop(frames=lambda: {"wrist_l": cyc_f.next(), "wrist_r": cyc_f.next()}, guard_judge=timed_guard,
+                      grippers=lambda: {"right": {"opening": 0.1}})
+        fl.attempt_start("pick_ball", 1e9, None, guarded=True)
+
+        def live_guard_tick():
+            for role in ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"):
+                timed_guard({role: cyc_g.next()})
+        plan = load_plan(ROOT / "plans" / "hill_climb_v4.yaml")
+        mv = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=lambda payload: {"content": []})
+        mv.annotator = det.annotate_frames
+        stage = next(st for st in plan.stages if plan.guard is not None and plan.guard.covers(st.name))
+        cyc_m, t_el = Cycle(wrists), [0.0]
+
+        def monitor_tick():
+            t_el[0] += 3.0
+            mv._build(stage, {"scene": others[0], "wrist_l": cyc_m.next(), "wrist_r": cyc_m.next()}, t_el[0])
+        threads = [threading.Thread(target=periodic(0.1, live_guard_tick), daemon=True),
+                   threading.Thread(target=periodic(0.05, fl.step, True), daemon=True),
+                   threading.Thread(target=periodic(3.0, monitor_tick), daemon=True)]
+    else:
+        def flat_out(k: int):
+            cyc = Cycle(wrists[k:] + wrists[:k])
+            while not stop.is_set():
+                timed_guard({"wrist_r": cyc.next()})
+        threads = [threading.Thread(target=flat_out, args=(k,), daemon=True) for k in range(args.threads)]
+
+    rows: list[dict] = []
+    t_end = time.perf_counter() + args.sustained
+    for th in threads:
+        th.start()
+    print(f"\nsustained {args.sustained_mode} load, {args.sustained:.0f} s"
+          f"{'' if args.sustained_mode == 'match' else f' on {args.threads} threads'}:")
+    print(f"{'window':>8} {'guard p50':>10} {'guard p95':>10} {'guards/s':>9} {'tick p95':>9} {'overruns':>9} "
+          f"{'cpu %':>7}  thermal")
+    w = 0
+    while time.perf_counter() < t_end:
+        c0, w0 = time.process_time(), time.perf_counter()
+        stop.wait(min(args.window, t_end - w0))
+        with lock:
+            g, tk, ov = rec["guard"], rec["tick"], over["n"]
+            rec["guard"], rec["tick"], over["n"] = [], [], 0
+        wall = time.perf_counter() - w0
+        row = {"t_s": round((w + 1) * args.window), "guards": len(g), "guards_per_s": len(g) / wall,
+               "guard_p50_ms": float(np.percentile(g, 50)) if g else None,
+               "guard_p95_ms": float(np.percentile(g, 95)) if g else None,
+               "tick_p95_ms": float(np.percentile(tk, 95)) if tk else None, "overruns": ov,
+               "cpu_pct": 100.0 * (time.process_time() - c0) / wall, "thermal": thermal_state()}
+        rows.append(row)
+        f = lambda v: f"{v:10.2f}" if v is not None else f"{'-':>10}"  # noqa: E731
+        print(f"{row['t_s']:>7}s {f(row['guard_p50_ms'])} {f(row['guard_p95_ms'])} {row['guards_per_s']:9.1f} "
+              f"{f(row['tick_p95_ms'])[1:]} {ov:9d} {row['cpu_pct']:7.1f}  {row['thermal'] or ''}")
+        w += 1
+    stop.set()
+    for th in threads:
+        th.join(timeout=2.0)
+    return rows
+
+
+def thermal_state() -> str | None:
+    """macOS: `pmset -g therm` (CPU_Speed_Limit 100 = not throttled). None elsewhere."""
+    if sys.platform != "darwin":
+        return None
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.run(["pmset", "-g", "therm"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2).stdout
+    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
+        return None
+    vals = [ln.strip().replace(" ", "") for ln in out.splitlines() if "=" in ln]
+    return ",".join(vals) or "no warning"
+
+
+def machine() -> dict[str, str | int | None]:
+    info: dict[str, str | int | None] = {"host": socket.gethostname(), "platform": platform.platform(),
+                                         "machine": platform.machine(), "python": platform.python_version(),
+                                         "cpus": os.cpu_count(), "numpy": np.__version__,
+                                         "pillow": Image.__version__}
+    try:
+        import scipy
+        info["scipy"] = scipy.__version__
+    except ImportError:
+        info["scipy"] = None
+    if sys.platform == "darwin":
+        for key in ("machdep.cpu.brand_string", "hw.perflevel0.physicalcpu", "hw.perflevel1.physicalcpu"):
+            try:
+                info[key] = subprocess.run(["sysctl", "-n", key], capture_output=True, text=True,
+                                           timeout=2).stdout.strip()
+            except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
+                pass
+    else:
+        info["cpu"] = platform.processor()
+    info["threads_env"] = {k: os.environ.get(k) for k in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS",
+                                                          "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS")}
+    return info
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n\n")[0])
+    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=Path, default=ROOT / "tests" / "data" / "guard_frames",
+                    help="folder of recorded JPEGs (names with 'wrist' are wrist views)")
+    ap.add_argument("--config", type=Path, default=ROOT / "plans" / "table_colors.yaml")
+    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=float, default=2.0, help="time per component")
+    ap.add_argument("--sustained", type=float, default=0.0, help="seconds of sustained load (300 = 5 min)")
+    ap.add_argument("--sustained-mode", choices=("match", "max"), default="match")
+    ap.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=4, help="--sustained-mode max: guard threads")
+    ap.add_argument("--window", type=float, default=30.0, help="sustained: seconds per row")
+    ap.add_argument("--no-ref", action="store_true", help="skip the pre-optimisation detector")
+    ap.add_argument("--label", default="", help="free text saved in the JSON (e.g. 'on battery, lid open')")
+    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None)
+    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
+
+    wrists, others = _load_frames(args.frames)
+    det = TableDetector.from_file(args.config)
+    ref = None if args.no_ref else _reference_detector(args.config)
+    info = machine()
+    print(f"{info['host']}  {info['platform']}  {info['machine']}  python {info['python']}  "
+          f"numpy {info['numpy']}  pillow {info['pillow']}  scipy {info['scipy']}")
+    print(f"{len(wrists)} wrist frames, {len(others)} other frames from {args.frames}")
+    t0 = time.time()
+    comp = components(args, det, ref, wrists, others)
+    print(f"\n{'component':<14} {'p50 ms':>9} {'p95 ms':>9} {'max ms':>9} {'cpu %':>7} {'n':>7}")
+    for name, r in comp.items():
+        print(f"{name:<14} {r['p50_ms']:9.3f} {r['p95_ms']:9.3f} {r['max_ms']:9.3f} {r['cpu_pct']:7.1f} {r['n']:7d}")
+    if "guard_ref" in comp:
+        print(f"\nguard speedup over the pre-optimisation detector: "
+              f"p50 x{comp['guard_ref']['p50_ms'] / comp['guard']['p50_ms']:.2f}, "
+              f"p95 x{comp['guard_ref']['p95_ms'] / comp['guard']['p95_ms']:.2f}")
+    # What the match asks of the guard: 10 Hz live guard + 20 Hz fast loop, two wrists each = 60 frames/s worst case
+    load = 60 * comp["guard"]["mean_ms"] / 1000
+    print(f"match guard load (60 wrist frames/s): ~{100 * load:.0f}% of one core")
+    rows = sustained(args, det, wrists, others) if args.sustained > 0 else []
+    result = {"machine": info, "label": args.label, "started": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z",
+                                                                             time.localtime(t0)),
+              "frames": str(args.frames), "components": comp, "match_guard_core_fraction": load,
+              "sustained": {"mode": args.sustained_mode, "seconds": args.sustained, "rows": rows} if rows else None}
+    out = args.out or ROOT / "bench_results" / (f"mac_bench_{info['host']}_"
+                                                f"{time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S', time.localtime(t0))}.json")
+    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    print(f"\nwrote {out}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+"""Build the start-pose reference for harness.start_pose from LeRobot teleop datasets.
+
+    python tools/start_poses.py OUT.json DATASET_DIR [DATASET_DIR ...]
+
+For every episode, the first frame of each task segment (a change of task_index starts a segment) becomes one
+reference start under that task's exact text. observation.state is [left 6 joints, left gripper, right 6 joints,
+right gripper] (the Metal/YAM follower layout in meta/info.json); grippers are dropped. Needs pyarrow.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+def segment_starts(dataset: Path) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
+    import pyarrow.parquet as pq  # noqa: PLC0415 - only this tool needs it
+
+    info = json.loads((dataset / "meta" / "info.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    names = info["features"]["observation.state"]["names"]
+    idx = {side: [names.index(f"{side}_joint_{j}") for j in range(6)] for side in ("left", "right")}
+    tasks = {}
+    for line in (dataset / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+        if line.strip():
+            t = json.loads(line)
+            tasks[t["task_index"]] = t["task"]
+    out = []
+    for f in sorted((dataset / "data").glob("chunk-*/episode_*.parquet")):
+        cols = pq.read_table(f, columns=["observation.state", "task_index"]).to_pydict()
+        prev, seg = None, 0
+        for state, ti in zip(cols["observation.state"], cols["task_index"]):
+            if ti != prev:
+                start = {side: [round(float(state[i]), 4) for i in ids] for side, ids in idx.items()}
+                start["episode"] = f"{dataset.name}/{f.stem}/seg{seg}"
+                out.append((tasks[ti], start))
+                prev, seg = ti, seg + 1
+    return out
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    if len(argv) < 2:
+        print(__doc__)
+        return 2
+    by: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
+    for d in argv[1:]:
+        for task, start in segment_starts(Path(d)):
+            by.setdefault(task, []).append(start)
+    Path(argv[0]).write_text(json.dumps({"by_prompt": by}, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    for task, starts in by.items():
+        print(f"{len(starts):3d}  {task}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
diff --git a/tools/trace_report.py b/tools/trace_report.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0183e6d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/trace_report.py
@@ -0,0 +1,170 @@
+"""Readable trace of harness runs: one page per run (what was asked, what the monitor saw, why each stage ended)
+and an index of all runs.
+
+    python tools/trace_report.py                 # every run under runs/, then opens runs/index.html
+    python tools/trace_report.py runs/<stamp>    # one run
+
+Each run page lists, in time order: every prompt sent to Dream (and why), every monitor verdict with its reason,
+confidence and the frames it judged, attempt ends, skips, recoveries and the stop. Frames are the run's own files.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import html
+import json
+import pathlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime
+
+RUNS = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "runs"
+
+CSS = """
+:root { --bg:#fbfbfa; --text:#1d1d1b; --muted:#6b6b66; --line:#e4e4df; --ok:#2f7d4f; --bad:#b3261e; --prompt:#1f5fa8;
+  --rewrite-bg:#fff4dc; --rewrite:#8a5a00; --op-bg:#eef1ff; --op:#3b3fa8; }
+@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root { --bg:#161615; --text:#ecece8; --muted:#9b9b95; --line:#30302c;
+  --ok:#5cc28a; --bad:#ff6b5e; --prompt:#7fb0ff; --rewrite-bg:#2e2412; --rewrite:#f0b54a; --op-bg:#1d2033; --op:#a9adff; } }
+* { box-sizing:border-box; }
+body { margin:0; padding:24px 16px 64px; background:var(--bg); color:var(--text);
+  font:14px/1.5 Inter, system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
+main { max-width:1100px; margin:0 auto; }
+h1 { font-size:20px; margin:0 0 4px; } h2 { font-size:15px; margin:28px 0 8px; }
+.muted { color:var(--muted); } a { color:inherit; }
+table { border-collapse:collapse; width:100%; } td, th { text-align:left; padding:6px 8px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
+  vertical-align:top; } th { color:var(--muted); font-weight:500; }
+.ok { color:var(--ok); font-weight:600; } .bad { color:var(--bad); font-weight:600; }
+.row { display:grid; grid-template-columns:64px 130px 1fr; gap:12px; padding:10px 0; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line); }
+.t { color:var(--muted); } .kind { font-weight:600; }
+.prompt { color:var(--prompt); } .stage { border-top:2px solid var(--text); margin-top:18px; }
+.thumbs { display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px; flex-wrap:wrap; }
+.thumbs img { height:96px; border-radius:4px; display:block; }
+.filter { margin:8px 0 0; } .filter label { margin-right:14px; }
+body.hide-wait .waiting { display:none; }
+.rewrite { background:var(--rewrite-bg); } .rewrite .kind { color:var(--rewrite); }
+.op { background:var(--op-bg); } .op .kind { color:var(--op); }
+.was { color:var(--muted); text-decoration:line-through; }
+"""
+
+KINDS = {"stage_start": "stage", "start": "prompt", "set_prompt": None, "prompt_change": "prompt", "verdict": "verdict",
+         "attempt_end": "attempt end", "stage_skipped": "skipped", "recovery": "recovery", "retry": "retry",
+         "entry_check": "entry check", "stop": "stop", "run_end": "run end", "guard_stop": "guard stop",
+         "prompt_rewrite": "monitor rewrote prompt", "tower_prompt": "tower prompt", "reset": "reset",
+         "auto_reset": "auto reset", "station_restart": "station restart", "operator_input": "operator said",
+         "operator_reply": "claude replied", "operator_action": "operator action", "guard_tripped": "guard tripped"}
+
+
+def _when(stamp: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        return datetime.strptime(stamp, "%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ").strftime("%d %b %H:%M UTC")
+    except ValueError:
+        return stamp
+
+
+def render(run: pathlib.Path) -> dict:
+    events = [json.loads(l) for l in (run / "events.jsonl").read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
+    if not events:
+        return {}
+    t0 = events[0]["t"]
+    summary = json.loads((run / "summary.json").read_text()) if (run / "summary.json").exists() else {}
+    end = next((e for e in reversed(events) if e["event"] in ("run_end", "stop")), {})
+    task = next((e.get("task") for e in events if e["event"] == "run_start"), "")
+    rows = []
+    for e in events:
+        kind = KINDS.get(e["event"])
+        if kind is None:
+            continue
+        t = f"+{e['t'] - t0:.0f}s"
+        stage = e.get("stage") or ""
+        body = ""
+        cls = "row"
+        if e["event"] == "stage_start":
+            cls += " stage"
+            body = f"<b>{html.escape(stage)}</b> <span class='prompt'>{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}</span>"
+        elif e["event"] == "prompt_rewrite":
+            cls += " rewrite"
+            body = (f"<span class='was'>“{html.escape(e.get('previous') or '')}”</span><br>"
+                    f"<span class='prompt'>“{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}”</span>"
+                    f"<div class='muted'>because: {html.escape(str(e.get('reason') or ''))}</div>")
+        elif e["event"] in ("operator_input", "operator_reply", "operator_action"):
+            cls += " op"
+            if e["event"] == "operator_input":
+                body = f"“{html.escape(e.get('text') or '')}”"
+            elif e["event"] == "operator_reply":
+                acts = e.get("actions") or {}
+                body = html.escape(str(e.get("reply") or "")) + (
+                    f"<div class='muted'>{html.escape(json.dumps(acts))}</div>" if acts else "")
+            else:
+                body = html.escape(f"{e.get('action')}: {e.get('note') or e.get('stages') or e.get('stage') or ''}")
+        elif e["event"] == "prompt_change" and str(e.get("why") or "").startswith("monitor next_prompt"):
+            continue   # shown by its prompt_rewrite row
+        elif e["event"] in ("start", "prompt_change"):
+            why = e.get("why")
+            body = f"<span class='prompt'>“{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}”</span>" + (
+                f" <span class='muted'>({html.escape(str(why))})</span>" if why else "")
+        elif e["event"] in ("verdict", "entry_check"):
+            done = e.get("done")
+            if e.get("reason") == "monitor call in flight":
+                cls += " waiting"
+            mark = "<span class='ok'>done</span>" if done else "<span class='muted'>not yet</span>" if done is None \
+                else "<span class='bad'>failing</span>"
+            conf = e.get("confidence")
+            body = f"{mark} <span class='muted'>{'' if conf is None else f'conf {conf:.2f}'}</span> " \
+                   f"{html.escape(str(e.get('reason') or ''))}"
+            frames = e.get("frames") or []
+            if frames:
+                body += "<div class='thumbs'>" + "".join(
+                    f"<a href='{html.escape(f)}' target='_blank'><img loading='lazy' src='{html.escape(f)}' "
+                    f"title='{html.escape(f)}'></a>" for f in frames) + "</div>"
+        elif e["event"] == "attempt_end":
+            ok = e.get("ok")
+            body = ("<span class='ok'>passed</span> " if ok else "<span class='bad'>ended</span> ") + \
+                html.escape(str(e.get("reason") or ""))
+        else:
+            body = html.escape(str(e.get("reason") or e.get("why") or e.get("prompt") or e.get("mode") or ""))
+        rows.append(f"<div class='{cls}'><div class='t'>{t}</div><div class='kind'>{html.escape(kind)}"
+                    f"<div class='muted'>{html.escape(stage)}</div></div><div>{body}</div></div>")
+    stages = summary.get("stages") or []
+    stage_rows = "".join(
+        f"<tr><td>{html.escape(s['name'])}</td><td class='{'ok' if s.get('ok') else 'bad'}'>"
+        f"{'passed' if s.get('ok') else 'no'}</td><td>{s.get('attempts') or 0}</td>"
+        f"<td>{round(s.get('seconds') or 0)} s</td><td class='muted'>{html.escape(str(s.get('reason') or ''))[:160]}</td></tr>"
+        for s in stages if s.get("attempts"))
+    secs = round(end.get("seconds") or (events[-1]["t"] - t0))
+    page = f"""<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><title>Run {run.name}</title><style>{CSS}</style></head>
+<body><main><p><a href="../index.html">All runs</a></p>
+<h1>Run {html.escape(run.name)}</h1>
+<div class="muted">{_when(run.name)} · {secs} s · ended: {html.escape(str(end.get('reason') or end.get('why') or '?'))}</div>
+<p class="muted">{html.escape(task or '')}</p>
+<h2>Stages</h2><table><tr><th>Stage</th><th>Passed</th><th>Attempts</th><th>Time</th><th>How it ended</th></tr>{stage_rows}</table>
+<h2>Timeline</h2>
+<div class="filter muted"><label><input type="checkbox" id="w" onchange="document.body.classList.toggle('hide-wait', this.checked)">
+hide "monitor call in flight"</label></div>
+{''.join(rows)}
+</main></body></html>"""
+    (run / "trace.html").write_text(page)
+    passed = [s["name"] for s in stages if s.get("ok")]
+    return {"run": run.name, "secs": secs, "end": str(end.get("reason") or end.get("why") or ""), "passed": passed,
+            "tried": [s["name"] for s in stages if s.get("attempts")]}
+
+
+def index(items: list[dict]) -> pathlib.Path:
+    rows = "".join(
+        f"<tr><td><a href='{i['run']}/trace.html'>{_when(i['run'])}</a></td><td>{i['secs']} s</td>"
+        f"<td class='ok'>{html.escape(', '.join(i['passed'])) or '<span class=muted>none</span>'}</td>"
+        f"<td class='muted'>{html.escape(i['end'])[:120]}</td></tr>" for i in items)
+    path = RUNS / "index.html"
+    path.write_text(f"""<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><title>Harness runs</title><style>{CSS}</style></head>
+<body><main><h1>Harness runs</h1><p class="muted">Newest first. Click a run for its timeline.</p>
+<table><tr><th>Run</th><th>Length</th><th>Stages passed</th><th>How it ended</th></tr>{rows}</table></main></body></html>""")
+    return path
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    targets = [pathlib.Path(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or sorted(
+        (p for p in RUNS.iterdir() if (p / "events.jsonl").exists()), reverse=True)
+    items = [i for i in (render(t) for t in targets) if i]
+    out = index(items) if not sys.argv[1:] else targets[0] / "trace.html"
+    print(out)
+    if sys.platform == "darwin":
+        subprocess.run(["open", str(out)], check=False)
